# Lazarus, Samuel & Near Death Experiences

**[00:00:00]** What does the parable of the rich man and Lazarus in Luke 16 have in common with King Saul summoning Samuel through a medium at Endor and near-death experiences?

**[00:00:14]** Today you'll find out. That's up next on the Dance of Life.

**[00:00:30]** Welcome, welcome everybody. Welcome to the show. This is the Dance of Life podcast and I'm your host, Tudor Alexander.

**[00:00:36]** Today we are wrapping up our afterlife series. We still have a few episodes to go. We have about two more to go, but nevertheless, we're at the home stretch now.

**[00:00:44]** We're doing some very interesting topics. Last time we talked about heaven, out-of-body experiences, ascension in the Bible, like with Elijah and Enoch.

**[00:00:53]** Of course, those things are very misinterpreted, just like everything else.

**[00:00:56]** And today we're looking at some other interesting things.

**[00:00:59]** With.

**[00:01:00]** Near-death experiences with the parable of the rich man and Lazarus and with Saul in the medium at Endor.

**[00:01:07]** So we have a lot of very interesting things to cover. I hope this will be very edifying for you.

**[00:01:12]** I announced this episode quite a while ago in the afterlife series at the very beginning because people were asking about what about the rich man and Lazarus?

**[00:01:19]** What about the rich man and Lazarus? What about Luke 16?

**[00:01:23]** And so I announced, I said, I'm going to have a dedicated episode just to this particular question.

**[00:01:30]** Because it is a very important question.

**[00:01:31]** And more importantly, it's you that you understand what the parable of the rich man and Lazarus is actually talking about.

**[00:01:39]** It's not talking about the afterlife.

**[00:01:40]** And if you realize that, if you realize what it's actually talking about and what it's pointing to, it gives you a profound new level of appreciation for Christ's words, for his intelligence and brilliance, his poetic mastery of language and how he uses parables to make a particular point.

**[00:02:00]** So that's what my goal is today with that particular section is that you marvel, right?

**[00:02:05]** Your theology should always lead you back to marveling.

**[00:02:07]** And usually what we want to do, like with everything else, is put it into a box that we can easily understand.

**[00:02:14]** Oh, it's just about the afterlife and just make it simple.

**[00:02:17]** Of course, it's not.

**[00:02:18]** And as a result, you miss out on such important lessons and spiritual teachings from Christ.

**[00:02:24]** So hopefully we can undo the deception today.

**[01:04:54]** He's at this social dinner, very high profile dinner,

**[01:05:00]** that you are, the parable of the wedding feast where the guests are making excuses.

**[01:05:11]** And they're making excuses about coming to the banquet,

**[01:05:23]** and not being in love with money and wealth and so on.

**[01:05:28]** All of these things are being spoken to at this dinner.
